Every later rule (null detection, type inference, statistics,\nfrequencies) works on the trimmed table this feature produces, so trimming happens here, before\nany other rule applies.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `internal/csvdata` exposes `Read(path string) (Table, error)`.\n- `Table` carries `Header []string` and `Records []Record`. `Record` carries `Line int` (the 1-based\n  line on which the record starts, as reported by `csv.Reader.FieldPos`) and `Fields []string`.\n- The file is read with `encoding/csv`. `FieldsPerRecord` is set to `-1` so that a record whose\n  width differs from the header is returned to the caller intact; the rejection rules are owned by\n  FEATURE-CSV-Rejection.md.\n- The first record is the header. Every header name and every value has leading and trailing\n  spaces trimmed (`strings.Trim(s, \" \")`) before any other rule.\n- Quoted fields decode per RFC 4180: a quoted field may contain commas, doubled quotes and newlines.\n- `Read` returns an error that wraps the underlying `*os.PathError` when the file cannot be opened\n  or read, so callers can tell an operational failure from a content problem.\n- After argument validation, `cli.Run` reads the file with `csvdata.Read`. A successfully read file\n  exits `0`; what is written to standard output is defined by later stories.\n\n## Required unit tests\n\nThe build agent writes these tests in `internal/csvdata` with exactly these names:\n\n- `TestReadTrimsHeaderAndValues`\n- `TestReadQuotedFieldWithComma`\n- `TestReadReturnsRaggedRecordsWithLineNumbers`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC import cli_runner\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    path = os.path.join(d, \"padded.csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\") as f:\n        f.write(\" id , name \\n 1 , Ada \\n 2 , Grace \\n\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(path)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0\n=== END AC csv-reading-valid-file-exits-zero ===\n\n=== AC or any supplied data file and never embeds an answer.\n- Trimming removes spaces only from the ends of a header name or value, never from its interior.\n\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Null-Values.md ===\n# FEATURE: Null Values\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Classify a trimmed value as null when it is empty or equals NA, N/A, or NULL ignoring case. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, FEATURE-CSV-Reading.md |\n| Provides    | csvdata.IsNull |\n| Consumes    | csvdata.Read |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nNull handling is one rule shared by every statistic: nulls count toward `rows` and `nulls` but are\nignored by `distinct`, `min`, `max`, `sum`, `mean`, `median`, type inference and frequencies.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `internal/csvdata` exposes `IsNull(value string) bool`, applied to already-trimmed values.\n- It returns true for the empty string and for `NA`, `N/A` and `NULL` in any letter case.\n- It returns false for every other value, including `0`, `nan`, `none`, `n/a/` and `NAN`.\n- No other package re-implements the null rule; all call `csvdata.IsNull`.\n\n## Required unit tests\n\nThe build agent writes these tests in `internal/csvdata` with exactly these names:\n\n- `TestIsNullEmptyAndSentinels` covers the empty string and every case variant of `NA`, `N/A`, `NULL`.\n- `TestIsNullRejectsLookalikes` covers `0`, `nan`, `none`, `NAN`, `nul` and `n/a/`.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC null-values-sentinels ===\nIntent: Empty and the NA, N/A, NULL sentinels in any case are null.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/csvdata\", \"TestIsNullEmptyAndSentinels\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC null-values-sentinels ===\n\n=== AC null-values-lookalikes ===\nIntent: Values that merely resemble a null sentinel are not null.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/csvdata\", \"TestIsNullRejectsLookalikes\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC null-values-lookalikes ===\n\n=== AC null-values-file-with-nulls-reads ===\nIntent: A file containing null sentinels of every spelling is read by the built executable without error.\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nsentinels = [\"\", \"NA\", \"na\", \"N/A\", \"n/a\", \"NULL\", \"null\"]\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    path = os.path.join(d, \"nulls.csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\") as f:\n        f.write(\"id,v\\n\")\n        for i, s in enumerate(sentinels):\n            f.write(f\"{i},{s}\\n\")\n    code, out, err = BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-CSV-Reading.md ===\n# FEATURE: CSV Reading\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Read a UTF-8 RFC 4180 CSV file with encoding/csv and trim every header name and value of leading and trailing spaces. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, FEATURE-CLI-Arguments.md |\n| Provides    | csvdata.Read |\n| Consumes    | internal/cli.Run |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`csvstat` profiles a CSV file. Every later rule (null detection, type inference, statistics,\nfrequencies) works on the trimmed table this feature produces, so trimming happens here, before\nany other rule applies.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `internal/csvdata` exposes `Read(path string) (Table, error)`.\n- `Table` carries `Header []string` and `Records []Record`. `Record` carries `Line int` (the 1-based\n  line on which the record starts, as reported by `csv.Reader.FieldPos`) and `Fields []string`.\n- The file is read with `encoding/csv`. `FieldsPerRecord` is set to `-1` so that a record whose\n  width differs from the header is returned to the caller intact; the rejection rules are owned by\n  FEATURE-CSV-Rejection.md.\n- The first record is the header. Every header name and every value has leading and trailing\n  spaces trimmed (`strings.Trim(s, \" \")`) before any other rule.\n- Quoted fields decode per RFC 4180: a quoted field may contain commas, doubled quotes and newlines.\n- `Read` returns an error that wraps the underlying `*os.PathError` when the file cannot be opened\n  or read, so callers can tell an operational failure from a content problem.\n- After argument validation, `cli.Run` reads the file with `csvdata.Read`. A successfully read file\n  exits `0`; what is written to standard output is defined by later stories.\n\n## Required unit tests\n\nThe build agent writes these tests in `internal/csvdata` with exactly these names:\n\n- `TestReadTrimsHeaderAndValues`\n- `TestReadQuotedFieldWithComma`\n- `TestReadReturnsRaggedRecordsWithLineNumbers`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC FEATURE-Null-Values.md ===\n# FEATURE: Null Values\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Classify a trimmed value as null when it is empty or equals NA, N/A, or NULL ignoring case. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, FEATURE-CSV-Reading.md |\n| Provides    | csvdata.IsNull |\n| Consumes    | csvdata.Read |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nNull handling is one rule shared by every statistic: nulls count toward `rows` and `nulls` but are\nignored by `distinct`, `min`, `max`, `sum`, `mean`, `median`, type inference and frequencies.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- `internal/csvdata` exposes `IsNull(value string) bool`, applied to already-trimmed values.\n- It returns true for the empty string and for `NA`, `N/A` and `NULL` in any letter case.\n- It returns false for every other value, including `0`, `nan`, `none`, `n/a/` and `NAN`.\n- No other package re-implements the null rule; all call `csvdata.IsNull`.\n\n## Required unit tests\n\nThe build agent writes these tests in `internal/csvdata` with exactly these names:\n\n- `TestIsNullEmptyAndSentinels` covers the empty string and every case variant of `NA`, `N/A`, `NULL`.\n- `TestIsNullRejectsLookalikes` covers `0`, `nan`, `none`, `NAN`, `nul` and `n/a/`.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC null-values-sentinels ===\nIntent: Empty and the NA, N/A, NULL sentinels in any case are null.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/csvdata\", \"TestIsNullEmptyAndSentinels\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC null-values-sentinels ===\n\n=== AC null-values-lookalikes ===\nIntent: Values that merely resemble a null sentinel are not null.\n\nfrom drydock_go import run_test\n\nresult = run_test(\"./internal/csvdata\", \"TestIsNullRejectsLookalikes\")\nprint(result.stdout)\nassert result.passed, (result.status, result.stdout, result.stderr)\n=== END AC null-values-lookalikes ===\n\n=== AC null-values-file-with-nulls-reads ===\nIntent: A file containing null sentinels of every spelling is read by the built executable without error.\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nsentinels = [\"\", \"NA\", \"na\", \"N/A\", \"n/a\", \"NULL\", \"null\"]\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    path = os.path.join(d, \"nulls.csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\") as f:\n        f.write(\"id,v\\n\")\n        for i, s in enumerate(sentinels):\n            f.write(f\"{i},{s}\\n\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(path)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0\n=== END AC null-values-file-with-nulls-reads ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- A value is trimmed before it is tested; a value of only spaces is therefore null.\n- `0`, `nan` and `none` are never null.\n\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-CSV-Rejection.md ===\n# FEATURE: CSV Rejection\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Reject an empty file, an empty or repeated header name, or a ragged record with exit 3, no standard output and one diagnostic line. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, FEATURE-CSV-Reading.md |\n| Provides    | rejected-input exit 3 |\n| Consumes    | csvdata.Read |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nA malformed file is rejected as a whole. Nothing is profiled and nothing reaches standard output,\nso a caller never mistakes partial output for a result.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- After reading, `cli.Run` validates the table and exits `3` with nothing on standard output when:\n  - the file has no records at all (zero bytes, or only blank content);\n  - a header name is empty after trimming (including a header of only spaces);\n  - a header name appears more than once (exact string comparison after trimming);\n  - any data record has a field count different from the header;\n  - `encoding/csv` reports a syntax error (for example a bare quote); this is rejected content, so\n    it is exit `3`, not `1`.\n- Exactly one diagnostic line is written to standard error. It names the file and, for a ragged\n  record, the record's line number (`Record.Line`).\n- A header followed by no data records is valid and is not rejected.\n- Exit `1` (unreadable file) is owned by FEATURE-Read-Failure.md; exit `2` by the CLI features.\n\n## Required unit tests\n\nThe build agent writes this test in `internal/cli` with exactly this name:\n\n- `TestRejectionDiagnosticNamesFileAndLine` runs `Run` with `bytes.Buffer` writers on a ragged file\n  and asserts stdout is empty, stderr is a single line, and that line contains the file path and the\n  line number of the ragged record.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC csv-rejection-ragged-supplied-file ===\nIntent: The supplied ragged file is rejected with exit 3 and nothing on standard output.\n\nimport os\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\npath = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(\"sources\", \"ragged.csv\"))\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 3\nassert out == \"\"\n=== END AC csv-rejection-ragged-supplied-file ===\n\n=== AC csv-rejection-empty-file ===\nIntent: A zero-byte file is rejected with exit 3 and nothing on standard output.\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    path = os.path.join(d, \"empty.csv\")\n    open(path, \"w\").close()\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(path)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 3\n    assert out == \"\"\n=== END AC csv-rejection-empty-file ===\n\n=== AC csv-rejection-empty-header-name   assert out == \"\"\n=== END AC csv-rejection-repeated-header-name ===\n\n=== AC csv-rejection-short-and-long-records ===\nIntent: A record with too few or too many fields is rejected with exit 3 and |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Exit 1 with nothing on standard output when the file cannot be opened or read. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, FEATURE-CSV-Rejection.md |\n| Provides    | operational-failure exit 1 |\n| Consumes    | csvdata.Read |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nAn operational failure (the file is unavailable) is distinguished from rejected content so callers\ncan tell a missing input from a malformed one.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- When `csvdata.Read` fails to open or read the path (nonexistent path, a directory, no\n  permission), `cli.Run` exits `1`.\n- Nothing is written to standard output; one diagnostic line naming the file is written to standard\n  error.\n- Exit `1` is reserved for this case. Content that was read but rejected is exit `3`\n  (FEATURE-CSV-Rejection.md); usage errors are exit `2` (FEATURE-CLI-Arguments.md and\n  FEATURE-CLI-Option-Validation.md).\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC read-failure-missing-file ===\nIntent: A path that does not exist exits 1 with nothing on standard output.\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    missing = os.path.join(d, \"no-such-file.csv\")\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(missing)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 1\n    assert out == \"\"\n=== END AC read-failure-missing-file ===\n\n=== AC read-failure-directory ===\nIntent: A path that is a directory cannot be read and exits 1 with nothing on standard output.\n\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(d)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 1\n    assert out == \"\"\n=== END AC read-failure-directory ===\n\n=== AC read-failure-distinct-from-rejection ===\nIntent: A readable but ragged file exits 3 while an unreadable one exits 1, so the two codes are not conflated.\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nragged = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(\"sources\", \"ragged.csv\"))\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:\n    missing = os.path.join(d, \"absent.csv\")\n    rejected_code, _, _ = cli_runner(ragged)\n    failed_code, out, err = cli_runner(missing)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert rejected_code == 3\n    assert failed_code == 1\n    assert failed_code != rejected_code\n=== END AC read-failure-distinct-from-rejection ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Exit `1` never Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Emit the profile header and one row per column in file order, filling column and rows. |\n| Depends On  | ARCHITECTURE.md, FEATURE-CSV-Reading.md, FEATURE-Null-Values.md, FEATURE-CSV-Rejection.md, FEATURE-CLI-Option-Validation.md |\n| Provides    | profile output header |\n| Consumes    | csvdata.Read, Later stories fill the remaining fields, so the output\nshape is fixed here and never changes.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Without `--freq`, standard output is CSV written by one `encoding/csv` writer with `\\n` line\n  endings.\n- The first row is the header `column,type,rows,nulls,distinct,min,max,sum,mean,median` (ten fields).\n- One row follows for each header column, in file order. Every row has ten fields.\n- `column` is the trimmed header name. `rows` is the number of data records (not counting the\n  header), written as a plain integer; a header-only file gives `0` for every column.\n- The fields `type`, `nulls`, `distinct`, `min`, `max`, `sum`, `mean` and `median` are owned by\n  later stories (Type-*, Profile-Counts, Profile-Extremes, Profile-Numeric-Stats). Until a later\n  story is built, the field is written empty. The final application fills all of them.\n- Exit `0` on success.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC profile-rows-header-and-columns-in-file-order ===\nIntent: The profile starts with the ten-field header and lists one row per input column in file order.\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\ndata = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(\"sources\", \"employees.csv\"))\nwith open(data, newline=\"\") as f:\n    source = list(csv.reader(f))\nnames = source[0]\ncode, out, err = cli_runner(data)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0\nparsed = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert parsed[0] == [\"column\", \"type\", \"rows\", \"nulls\", \"distinct\", \"min\", \"max\", \"sum\", \"mean\", \"median\"]\nassert [row[0] for row in parsed[1:]] == names\nassert all(len(row) == len(parsed[0]) for row in parsed)\n=== END AC profile-rows-header-and-columns-in-file-order ===\n\n=== AC profile-rows-count-matches-records ===\nIntent:
